title: "Cohezion Self-Improvement Backlog — single-loop, multi-thread"
created: 2026-06-05
owner: "/loop (self-paced, one item per tick)"
policy:
  - "One item per tick. Pick the lowest-numbered TODO whose deps are met."
  - "Every item: TDD red→green, a FALSIFIABLE check (must be able to come back negative),"
  - "  non-destructive/additive, respect calibrated invariants (.claude/rules/harness.md:"
  - "  A3/A4/A5/CA1/CC2/LM6 + the new K1/rule-5 OOM gates). ruff + targeted tests green."
  - "Commit surgically (explicit paths, churn-guard). Mark the item DONE here with the hash."
  - "Behavior-changing items (B3/B5/B6): land behind the falsifiable check; if it can't come"
  - "  back negative, downgrade to report-only and flag for human review instead."
  - "If a tick would violate a calibrated invariant or needs a human decision, STOP + surface it."
threads:
  A: "Audit (new report-only dimensions)"
  B: "Remediations (flagged findings → committed fixes)"
  C: "Self-improvement (routing feedback → fleet tunes itself — principle #3)"

## Notes
- **Elegant simplicity (audit dimension, 2026-06-06, user request):** the audit loop measures the structural COST of code — control-flow complexity (item 43), needless indirection (item 44), cohesion/LCOM4 (item 10), reachability (the wiring sweep) — and flags OUTLIERS for human/build-loop judgment. Guardrails: (a) OBJECTIVE metrics only, no subjective "elegance score" (false precision on a subjective thing — a number is a smell, not a verdict); (b) REPORT-ONLY, never auto-refactor (behavior-changing/destructive, against the non-destructive policy); (c) it is the DUAL of the wiring loop's "no fake edge" rule — complexity, like an import edge, must EARN ITS KEEP; ceremony without function is the opposite of simplicity.
- Items 1–5 are additive/safe; 6–8 are behavior-changing (land behind the falsifiable check, else report-only).
- **Scope-expansion directive (2026-06-06):** the loop no longer terminates at a fixed list. Each tick, after completing one item, it APPENDS ≥1 new falsifiable item that extends a thread into adjacent territory (a new `Task` slot, an unused neuron `country`, a new measurement, a new resilience/telemetry check, or the next wiring package). New items MUST be additive + falsifiable + invariant-respecting + non-fabricated (research-gated ids stay TODO until the research loop verifies them). The loop only STOPS/notifies when scope cannot be expanded without a human decision (every frontier item is BLOCKED on human input).
- **Loop-doctrine directives (2026-06-07, user):** four standing changes to how the loops run.
  (1) **Every interaction feeds a loop** — each user interaction (a question, a "consider <link>", a bug report, a directive) must be routed INTO an existing loop (build/wiring/research/remediation) or SEED a NEW loop, never handled one-off-and-forgotten. The artifact: a backlog item, a feed round, a ledger entry, a remediation finding, or a new loop arm. (2) **"Just about infinite loops"** — the loops are standing/recurring and self-expanding (scope-expansion + remediation arms); they only halt for a human-gated decision, then resume. (3) **Polyglot refactor as a loop arm** — item 124 (report-only, per-language native tools, non-destructive). (4) **Safeguards: adhere AND improve** — every tick respects the calibrated invariants (A3/A4/A5/CA1/CC2/LM6 + K1/rule-5 OOM + non-destructive wiring + surgical commits) AND may PROPOSE a better safeguard (a new harness invariant, a tighter resource gate, a new structural-before-behavioral check) as an additive, falsifiable item — the resource-aware router (item 122) is the first such self-authored safeguard (it would have prevented the fleet-saturation that broke the bot). Safeguard improvements are themselves additive + report-only-until-proven; never weaken an existing invariant without a human decision. (5) **Usage throttle — never run out of Claude (item 134, 2026-06-07)** — at the START of every self-paced loop tick, run `python scripts/loop_usage_guard.py` and OBEY the action: `proceed` → normal; `throttle` → widen the ScheduleWakeup delay (≥3600s) and shift inference to the local fleet (`extend_claude`) instead of spending agent turns; `halt` → do NOT re-arm ScheduleWakeup (stop autonomous wakeups; only user-driven turns + local-fleet work continue), and PushNotification the user. This gates the Claude-Code plan quota (the session/weekly limits), NOT the API \$ budget. The whole point of the great local inference we keep improving is that it's the escape valve — throttling means MORE local, never a hard stop on capability. (6) **RSI safeguard grounding (Anthropic Institute "When AI Builds Itself", 2026-06-07, FEED Round 32)** — these loops sit at the piece's *present* stage (agents write/edit/run code + delegate), NOT autonomous successor-design; calibrate claims accordingly. Its central risk — misalignment/error COMPOUNDING across self-improvement iterations — is the general form of the gaming we hit (loops validating each prior tick's inert output). The two safeguards it names are already our load-bearing ones: **human judgment on "which problems matter"** (= the money/deadline dominates, the human steers, Needs-human gates behavior changes) and **verification** (= the anti-gaming done-definition: a real non-test/non-`__init__` consumer + a behavior-asserting test). Treat these two as the non-negotiable RSI guards; a tick that would weaken either STOPS for a human.
- **Thread M — Multimodal local inference + self-improving local agent (2026-06-06, user directive: "make it part of the loop"):** items 83–90. The standing scope now includes multimodal local inference (vision INPUT already exists; image/audio/video OUTPUT + a self-improving local sub-agent are the frontier). VERIFIED real seams (no fabrication): `inference/registry.py` Task/Lane (extensible), `inference/fleet.py:415 _dispatch_one` (dispatcher branch-point), `audio/narrator.py::CosmoNarrator` + `audio/moshi_client.py` (audio-out + voices), `Task.VISION/EXTRACTION/OCR_DOC` (vision-in, LFM2.5-VL/PaddleOCR), `compound/executor.py inference_provider` + `scripts/hooks/experiential_learning_hook.py` + `inference/triune_orchestrator.py` (self-improvement path), strix-halo §2 sd.cpp-Vulkan-on-gfx1151 (image; Wan video). LOOP-BUILDABLE additive: 83 (Tasks), 84 (coverage report), 85 (narrator registration), 88 (closure instrument), 89 (I/O envelope). HUMAN/INFRA-GATED: 86 (image serving — lanes-up window + serving proof), 90 (agent wiring — behind 88's gate). RESEARCH-GATED: 87 (video — no verified artifact yet; the research loop must verify a real video GGUF before it leaves TODO; never invent a model to fill the slot).
- **Thread N — Model-roster governance (2026-06-06, user directive: "every model has a use-case, they compete for who wins, daily checks for better performers, validate fit"):** items 98–100. Backed by `docs/ops/MODEL_ROSTER_RECIPE_ASSESSMENT_2026-06-06.md` (28-model roster + recipes + omnirouter architecture). 98 = every served model → a use-case (inverse of item-62 gap); 99 = per-Task model tournament (generalizes RHO items 22/42/61 from harness-updates to MODELS); 100 = daily re-eval (needs-experiment — real serving + schedule + held-out set, never auto-swaps). Multimodal artifacts found ALREADY SERVED on :13305 → items 103 (AUDIO_TTS=kokoro, permissive) + 104 (IMAGE_GEN=SD-Turbo, license-gated) supersede items 85/86's serving-proof steps.
- **Thread Q — Quantum-accelerated physics (2026-06-06, user directive: "leverage bluequbit for novel physics simulations"):** item 101. VERIFIED access (`bluequbit` 0.18.5b1 + `qiskit` 2.4.0 installed, `QUANTUM_HACKATHON_PRIME.md`, 34 `physics/` modules). Research-gated: a real qiskit circuit + a verified BlueQubit job (creds/credit live-confirmed) BEFORE any behavior-change; classical sim always the default. Non-fabrication: name the exact physics module + circuit.
- **Thread P — Memory leverage / persistence (2026-06-06, user diagnostic: "are we capturing experiences + local inference in vault+SurrealDB? leveraging all memory? dynamic indexing?"):** items 106–108. LIVE FINDING: capture ≫ distillation ≫ recall. Raw is rich (journey_point=278741, vault=12338 notes); distillation is sparse (neuron=18, learnings=10, compound_learnings=2, mem0=4 — a ~15000:1 raw:distilled ratio, the bottleneck); recall exists (`vault_search_executor`/`skill_selector`/`request_alignment_analyzer`) but the BUILD loop uses only FLUME geometric code-recall, never the vault/neurons. Indexing is fragmented (Vexor `~/.vexor` code-index + semantic cache CA1/CA2 nomic-embed 768D [the dynamic L2] + FLUME 256D + SurrealDB — uncoordinated). 106 = measure utilization; 107 = bridge my retros (git-only) → vault/SurrealDB; 108 = wire vault/neuron recall into the loop. The capture WRITE (107) + recall WIRE (108) compose EXISTING read/write paths (`experiential_learning_hook` narrative_learning; the vault read-side) — non-fabricated, behavior-change gated behind the report-only first step.
- **Bot operational (item 102) + Kaggle (separate program):** the Hermes Telegram bot's inference path is VERIFIED local-first (Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-NoThinking on :13305, no empty-trap, clean tool-calls); the remaining work (item 102) is MCP-context accuracy + a real Telegram E2E send (human-in-the-loop). The "Kaggle continuations as part of the loop / start making money" directive belongs to the SEPARATE Kaggle program — see `~/.claude/rules/kaggle-portfolio.md` (Nemotron v10 live, 0.84 banked); folding continuations into the autonomous loop is tracked there, not duplicated here (non-fabrication: do not invent Kaggle items without the portfolio rule's verified state).
- **Local-silicon-first inference (2026-06-07, user steer):** every inference-bearing loop arm (research-mining/distillation, the HIHO-LM + AUTODQA quality gates, semantic-cache embeddings, advisory/multi-perspective review, any `extend_claude` sub-call) routes to the local fleet FIRST — `cohezion.inference.fleet.extend_claude` → router `:13305` (28-model catalog, on-demand) / NPU `:13306` (FLM tier + `nomic-embed-text-v2-moe-GGUF` 768D for CA1) — $0, escalate to cloud only on a quality-gate failure. VERIFIED live 2026-06-07: `:13305` serving 28 models, `:13306` NPU FLM + nomic-embed responding, `extend_claude` importable, ~59GiB free. HONEST scope: the deterministic AST-audit arm (items 43/44/47/63/64/97/105/110/111…) uses NO inference by design (a number is a smell, not an LLM verdict) — local models are NOT in that path, and the per-tick code-authoring is the cloud agent, not local silicon; do not overclaim otherwise. OOM discipline (rule K1 + live-topology): use ALREADY-LOADED models via the router; do NOT spin up the dedicated `:13307`(iGPU)/`:13309`(CPU) per-port servers (down & redundant — the router covers those tiers on demand; separate instances only ADD resident memory).
- **Loop ownership = the specialist team (2026-06-07, user directive "owned by agent specialist — all of the above"):** the loop is OWNED collectively by the Cohezion specialist agents, coordinated by **platform-coordinator**, with arms delegated by type: **compound-engineering-specialist** (build/AST + local-model optimization), **autoresearch-specialist** (experiment/measurement arms, fixed-budget validation), **swarm-orchestration-specialist** (multi-model debate/vote across NPU+iGPU+CPU for quality gates). Per-tick execution is DELEGATED to the owning specialist(s) via the Agent tool (context-efficient: work happens in the subagent, only the result returns); the main session is scheduler-of-record (ScheduleWakeup heartbeat). The deterministic AST-audit arm may still run inline (no inference); inference-bearing arms MUST be delegated to a specialist that routes through `:13305`.
- **Lemonade `:13305` router API (read 2026-06-07, https://lemonade-server.ai/docs/api/lemonade/):** all models are available on the ONE router — inference `POST /api/v1/chat/completions` + `POST /api/v1/embeddings` + `GET /api/v1/models` (auto-load on demand); management `POST /api/v1/load` (`model_name`, `ctx_size`, `llamacpp_backend`∈{vulkan,rocm,metal,cpu} selects the DEVICE, `llamacpp_args`), `/api/v1/unload`, `/api/v1/health`, `/api/v1/stats`, `/api/v1/system-info`, `/live`. Device map: NPU = `flm` recipe; iGPU = `llamacpp_backend: vulkan|rocm` (gfx1151); CPU = `cpu`. VERIFIED live 2026-06-07: 28-model catalog, NPU `llama3.2-1b-FLM` + iGPU `Granite-4.1-8B-GGUF` hot, system-info enumerates amd_npu(XDNA2 58 TOPS)+amd_gpu(gfx1151)+cpu(16C/32T). Prefer already-loaded models; `/api/v1/load` only to pre-warm a needed tier.
- **Retro + land-on-main flow (2026-06-07, user directive "retros + cherry-pick to main are part of the loop"):** each loop CYCLE (every ~3–5 ticks, or when a coherent unit of work is done, or at a `[retro:due]` signal) MUST: (1) write a brief retrospective to `docs/ops/learnings/RETRO-YYYY-MM-DD<letter>-<slug>.md` (frontmatter `date/kind: retro/thread/prompted_by/status`; honest — record failures + the honest scope, not a success narrative), evaluate `Skill(learn)` for a reusable skill, then `~/.claude/hooks/retro-watch.sh --clear`; and (2) LAND the cycle's clean commits on main — choosing the APPROPRIATE branch by commit type (verify first with `git cat-file -e origin/main:<file>`):
  - **Self-contained commit** (every file it touches ALREADY exists on `origin/main`): `git fetch origin`, create a FRESH branch off `origin/main` (`feat/<slug>`), `git cherry-pick <commit>` (already surgical/single-purpose), run harness verification (ruff format+check, targeted pytest), push, open a PR → `main`. Early-landing path. (Precedent: the 3 Anthropic-API cost commits cherry-picked main↦`ceabbb325..de0d5d0ac`.)
  - **Feature-dependent commit** (it EXTENDS a file that only exists on the feature branch — e.g. `simplicity_audit.py`/`problem_discovery.py`, which are feature-branch-only; the base `feat/adaptive-calibration-harness` is ~463 commits ahead of `origin/main`): do NOT cherry-pick onto main (it would conflict / create a broken half-file). Instead integrate the workbench into its feature base (`cz worktree sync` squash-merges `loop-backlog-build-0607` → `feat/adaptive-calibration-harness`); that feature branch lands on `main` as a UNIT via its own reviewed PR. Items 97/110/111… are this case.
  The worktree branch (`loop-backlog-build-0607`) is the WORKBENCH. GATE: remote push + PR is outward-facing — authorized within the loop for surgical commits, but `cz worktree sync` requires a CLEAN base (the main checkout currently has churn) so it is a CONFIRM-FIRST step; merges to `main` always go through PR/CI, never `--admin`.
